  - **Relabelling H3 "not supported (mixed)"** (F46): refuted; "Refuted" rests on N7's LoCoMo reader-swap tests, which survive Holm.
  - **The trace lock per file** (T18): measured 70–86 ms write waits; a lock-free scan with a locked re-read of the files that match keeps the wait under 10 ms.
- **Consequences:**
  - Code and tests: 81 files changed (not counting docs), 134 new test functions; 1,100 tests collected, 6 live tests deselected, 1,094 run offline, of which 22 are skipped without the external baselines' packages (18: A-MEM 3, Cognee 4, Graphiti 3, LangMem 3, Mem0 5) or a Qdrant server (4) and run in CI's new jobs instead; ruff and mypy pass. Every behaviour fix was checked to fail on the code before it.
  - **Evaluation requests and published numbers are unchanged:** `FROZEN_REQUESTS` in `tests/test_eval_leak.py` and `tests/test_frozen_config.py` are byte-for-byte the files of the round's base and pass; the golden cache-key tests in `tests/test_llm_client.py` and `tests/test_embedding.py` only gained tests; the default consolidation fingerprint stays `2f0adc7890b6a52e`. What these pins and the replays establish is: request bytes and cache keys are pinned; the N12 evaluation spaces were replayed read-only (D-069, and 853b0a1d above); the N2–N11 replay needs the original LLM cache, which the audit machine does not have (its cache starts on 2026-10-14), so it was not re-run (this corrects "the N2–N12 numbers still replay from data/cache" in D-050, D-068 and D-069).
  - **Requests that change on purpose:** none on the default or evaluation paths. Fewer requests in two places: forget no longer extracts `infer=False` sessions or re-sends sessions that moderation refused. A read-only replay may serve the fallback model's cached reply.
  - **Left for later or partial:**
    1. From T7: the header note of `docs/results/n9-final/tables.md` (written by `scripts/n9_tables.py`) still says only "nominal cost"; an amortised write-cost column needs the N9 runs (`evals.report.write_cost`);
    2. From T9: per-question outcome files for the N2–N11 runs; under the qwen preset, 853b0a1d (both models refused at build time, and refusals are not cached) and 7527f7e2 (a later primary reply in the cache shadows the fallback reply the build used) still do not replay read-only;
    3. From T15: the product UI still sends Gradio's analytics by default (`gr.Blocks` without `analytics_enabled=False` in `cuemem serve`, Docker and the demo), and test subprocesses are not guarded;
    4. From T12: many scoped keys at once, and the UI's event routes (the fixed 8 MiB tier, not counted per key), can still make a shared server hold a lot of parsed JSON: size its memory or lower `CUEMEM_MAX_BODY_BYTES`;
    5. From T19: the VectorStore lock held across Qdrant queries is the remaining cross-user stall on one process; prefix scans, the status listing, export and the delete-all forget still read every session;
    6. From T20 (found in passing): forget purges only the text embedder's query vectors, and the multimodal text-query vectors written by L1 (`mm|…|text|…`) appear not to be purged, which D-021 would require; this needs its own check;
    7. From T11 and D-069: local `Memory.search(mode="l9")` still runs L2 silently while HTTP returns 422; the keys file is not hot-reloaded and there is no command to mint keys;
    8. D-069's three remaining items (the nominal embedding cost, development/test twins, Chinese developer-facing text) are unchanged; the TypeScript half of its item 4 is done in reduced form (generated types), and MCP over HTTP behind scoped keys is done.
  - **Remaining limits:**
    - a forget that crashes after its commit leaves traces and query vectors uncleaned (the worker replays pending jobs on restart); one that fails after the network phase but before the commit leaves derived rows without vectors until the call is retried; an SDK `add` racing a forget on the same session is still possible; a rollback still bumps the forget epoch, which conservatively tells pending proxy and Agents retries that a forget happened;
    - cross-space lock cycles (a read on space X forgetting space Y) are not guarded;
    - per-space read gates and consolidation locks are never freed (about 2.4 KB per distinct space); a background consolidation that finishes just before `delete_space` can write a trace (counts and the space id only) after the cleanup; legacy orphan turn-number rows are cleaned only when that space next forgets or is deleted;
    - local-mode Qdrant still costs one open file per collection and RAM for every point; `meta.json` is rewritten on every create;
    - a half-written trace line is not truncated after a failed write;
    - adopting crash-left sessions, per-session turn numbers and orphan-vector deletion still assume one process writes a database at a time (as in D-068).
- **After the fourth audit round (D-071):**
  - **Group 2 (T5):** "nothing raises after a commit" now covers forget's trace rewrites and the embedding-cache purge too; before D-071, a trace rewrite that failed after forget's commit (a full disk, or a line that is not UTF-8) still raised.
  - **Group 2 (T14):** "(before, a remote run never ended until a restart …)" should read: before, a remote run was never ended by the server, and a restart did not end or adopt it either. Since D-071 runs survive a restart and serve hands the left-open ones over one idle period after start-up; a later add to an idle-closed run still reopens it, with the whole-run re-extraction and space replay of D-045.
  - **Group 4 (T12):** besides the pixel cap, images must be JPEG, PNG, WEBP, GIF, BMP or TIFF at ingest (a Pillow 12 XBM header let one scoped key stall the whole process); `image_data_url` stays unrestricted and byte-identical under Pillow 13.
  - **Group 7 (T15):** the `qdrant-server` job runs against a Qdrant server with an API key (`CUEMEM_TEST_QDRANT_API_KEY`, next to `CUEMEM_TEST_QDRANT_URL`); the "外部基线" workflow has still not run on GitHub.
  - **Left for later:** item 1 is done (the `tables.md` header note and a Holm-adjusted column, D-071 group 6) except the amortised write-cost column, which needs the N9 runs; item 3's analytics half is done (the UI sends no Gradio telemetry or version check unless `GRADIO_ANALYTICS_ENABLED=True`, and loads no Google fonts: a deliberate default change that affects no model request), while test subprocesses are still not guarded; item 6 is done (forget purges the multimodal query vectors and the vectors of deleted images no longer used).
  - **Remaining limits:** "a half-written trace line is not truncated after a failed write" is fixed (a failed append is truncated back; lines broken by earlier versions are kept as they are and no longer break forget or retention).

## D-071 The fourth audit round: after the repository went public, six lenses and 22 verified findings; evaluation requests, image requests and published numbers unchanged

- **Status:** adopted (after N12).
- **Context:**
  - The repository went public on GitHub. Its history was rewritten once before that, so commit hashes cited in older notes changed: the hashes in the docs and result records were updated, while `run.json` files in a local `runs/` directory still carry the old ones.
  - Going public changes who reads and runs the code: strangers install from the README, run the Docker image on a server, hand scoped keys to other people, and the authors of the external baselines read the comparisons. The fourth round therefore audited through six lenses aimed at that: open-source readiness and licences; CI and the supply chain; the code added in the third round; the security of a public deployment; the documentation and first run for an outsider; and the evaluation claims and how the research is presented. The rules were D-068's: read the code only, reproduce every candidate offline (FakeAPI, ScriptedModel, TestClient exploits, read-only copies of databases and caches), measure how often it happens, and say whether the fix would change an evaluation request.
  - Every candidate went through adversarial verification: another reviewer re-ran the reproduction, looked for counter-examples, narrowed the claim, rewrote the fix where needed (the "refined change") and judged it against the invariant. 22 findings were kept (P1–P22: 11 rated high, 10 medium, 1 low) and three proposals were dropped. Three findings were questions for the owner, whose decisions are binding below (P5: private vulnerability reporting through GitHub only; P15: no PyPI release for now); the third was settled by the owner before the round and needed no change here.
  - The fixes were implemented in two lanes and integrated with one merge commit (the one-line configuration error no longer echoes secrets such as the Qdrant key, the Docker notes mention the token-length check, and SECURITY.md's scope follows the new key limits). A review of the merged result through three lenses (core, security, supply chain) found 14 more problems: 11 were fixed, each behaviour fix with a test that fails before it, one is a documented residual (runs closed for idleness by the previous process, group 3), and two were documentation (closing `Memory` in plain scripts; the N12 records' pins), done in this docs pass. The groups below describe the state after that review.
  - The invariant is D-068's, with image bytes named explicitly: LLM and embedding requests under the default configuration and in evaluations stay byte-for-byte the same, and so do cache keys and the bytes of images sent to models; the frozen methods (B1, B2, B4-nobm25 = L1, B5 = L2, B6, and the three controller files) keep their behaviour; every behaviour fix has a regression test checked to fail on the code before it; every deliberate change of a default is listed in its group. No paid API was called and no evaluation was run.
- **Decision:**
  1. **Dependencies and the supply chain** (P1, P18, P5; revises D-068 groups 4 and 7, D-069 group 3, D-070 group 4):
     - **Pillow 13 with the same image bytes** (P1): the lock held Pillow 12.3.0 (41 advisories, 5 critical), certifi 2026.7.22 and brotli 1.2.0 (one critical advisory each) and filelock 4.1.1, all installed in the Docker image; gradio 6.30/6.31 and fastembed 0.9.0 declare `pillow<13`, so a plain upgrade did nothing. `[tool.uv] override-dependencies = ["pillow>=13.0.0"]` lifts the cap for uv and the image, while the core floor stays `pillow>=12.3.0`, so pip installs of `cuemem[ui]` still resolve 12.x; the lock bump changed exactly five packages (Pillow 13.0.0, certifi 2026.10.23, brotli 1.2.1, filelock 4.2.5, virtualenv 21.15.2, the last dev-only). Pillow 13 resizes vertically first when the height shrinks more than twice as much as the width ("Resizing vertically first" in its release notes), which changes the pixels of tall photos and so the cache keys of caption, multimodal-embedding and reader requests: `image_data_url` now resizes in two calls (width, then height), byte-identical to the single call on 12.3.0 and reproducing the 12.3.0 bytes on 13.0.0. The override also applies to `uv pip install` run inside the checkout, so an environment for comparing Pillow 12 must be built outside it or with `uv run --with pillow==12.3.0`.
     - **Only common image formats** (P1): `ACCEPTED_FORMATS` (JPEG, MPO included, PNG, WEBP, GIF, BMP and TIFF) is passed to `Image.open` at the untrusted probes (`MediaStore.put` and `check_image`, which cover `/api/v1`, `/api`, the UI, the proxy and MCP write-backs; import; the `/api/v1/media` Content-Type), never in `image_data_url`, which re-reads stored evaluation media. Before, one scoped key's 512-byte XBM header held the GIL for about 16 s (k = 28 on 12.3.0) and returned 500, stalling every tenant and route; now any other format, or a file that is not an image, gets ValueError "Not a valid image (accepted formats: …)" in under a second, 400 on `/api/v1` (was 500) and 409 on `/api`, also for pip users still on Pillow 12. AVIF, which opened before, is refused.
     - **CI** (P18): every action is pinned by commit SHA with a version comment (checkout v7.0.1 with `persist-credentials: false`, setup-uv v10.3.3, both on node24); uv is 0.12.24 in CI and in the Dockerfile (by image digest), inside the `uv_build` range, so the wheel is built with the bundled backend and no unhashed `uv-build` is fetched from PyPI (CI used to install an unverified latest uv); workflows declare read-only tokens, per-job timeouts (test 20, qdrant-server 10, package 10, docker 20, external baselines 90 minutes) and concurrency that cancels only superseded runs of the same pull request. `tests/test_ci_pins.py` checks all of this offline.
     - **Security policy and dependency alerts** (P5; owner decision): `SECURITY.md` names GitHub private vulnerability reporting as the only channel (no e-mail address), promises an acknowledgement within 7 days, defines the scope (serve with its tokens and the new key limits, MCP, the proxy, import and export, forget residue, the image and compose) and lists the maintainer steps that only an admin can take (private reporting, Dependabot alerts and security updates, push protection, and importing `.github/rulesets/protect-main.json`, unchanged and without bypass actors). `.github/dependabot.yml` covers GitHub Actions, `uv.lock` (minor and patch updates grouped, a 7-day cooldown) and the Dockerfile's base image; that docker entry stays dormant while `FROM python:3.12-slim` carries no digest (Dependabot only offers tags as precise as `3.12-slim`, and Python minor upgrades are ignored), so each image build pulls the current 3.12-slim, and the uv image in `COPY --from` is bumped by hand together with CI's setup-uv. A new "依赖审计" workflow, deliberately not a required check, runs `pip-audit` 2.10.1 on hashed exports of the image's dependencies and of all dependencies (push, pull request, weekly): on the round's base lock the image step reports 60 advisories in 4 packages, on the new lock none. The README points to SECURITY.md.
     - **Changed defaults:** the lock and the image install Pillow 13 and the patched certifi, brotli and filelock; images outside the six formats are refused at ingest (images already stored keep working, and `GET /api/v1/media` serves them as `application/octet-stream`); CI and the image use uv 0.12.24. New `cuemem.store.media.ACCEPTED_FORMATS`.
     - **Evaluation and images:** `tests/test_store.py::test_image_bytes_sent_to_models_are_unchanged` now pins `sha256(image_data_url(...))` (first 16 hex digits; the full digest for P01) at 768 and 512 pixels for all 24 demo images and 15 fixtures built without resampling (tall phone-shaped RGB, the modes L, LA, RGBA, P, 1, I;16 and CMYK, as PNG, JPEG, GIF, TIFF, BMP and WEBP, and a wide control), computed on Pillow 12.3.0. They pass on 13.0.0; with the old single resize, all 14 tall fixtures differ there. The verifier's 62 reference digests from 12.3.0 are identical under 13.0.0. This closes D-068's limit "B6a's photo path still has no offline request snapshot". The MemLens and N6 photo sets were not on the audit machines; comparing their digests under 12.3.0 and 13.0.0 is left to the owner, with identical digests expected.
  2. **Public deployment security** (P7, P13, P10, P4, P6, P22; revises D-056, D-062, D-068 group 4, D-069 group 6, D-070 groups 4 and 6):
     - **DNS rebinding without a token** (P7): `cuemem serve --insecure-no-auth` and `cuemem proxy --insecure-no-auth` on a non-loopback bind (every token-less Docker container) now accept as Host only IP literals, localhost, the bind hostname and the names in `CUEMEM_ALLOWED_HOSTS`; other names get 400 ("Invalid host header", or `V1ErrorBody` under `/api/v1`). Before, any Host was accepted there, because D-068's rationale assumed a token off loopback: a page on a rebinding domain could read, export and forget memories, spend the upstream key and call the UI's `/gradio_api` (reproduced: 200 with the stored PIN on 0.0.0.0 and 192.168.1.5, 400 on 127.0.0.1). Start-up names the accepted Hosts. Authenticated deployments, IP-literal access, localhost, the image's HEALTHCHECK and `CUEMEM_ALLOWED_HOSTS=*` are unchanged.
     - **Sign-in CSRF** (P7): `SameOriginOnly` also covers `/login` (and `/login/`, not `/logout`). Before, a cross-site form could sign a visitor into an attacker's scoped key, and the visitor's later input in the UI landed in the attacker's space. Cross-site and `Origin: null` sign-ins get 403; same-origin and Origin-less ones work as before.
     - **Weak tokens and plain HTTP** (P13): off loopback, `serve` and `proxy` refuse tokens shorter than 16 characters (`cuemem.api.auth.MIN_TOKEN_CHARS`), the admin token and plaintext scoped `token:` entries alike, every Docker container included: exit code 2 before the memory system opens, with a message that names the entry (never its value), the minimum, `openssl rand -hex 32` and the override `--insecure-weak-token`. Weak tokens are recorded by name when the keys are read (`Keys.weak`); `sha256:` entries cannot be measured and are not checked. The README's own example (`CUEMEM_API_TOKEN=your-token … --host 0.0.0.0`) used to start a public server. 16 rather than 24, and no placeholder blacklist: `secrets.token_urlsafe(16)` is 22 characters, and every placeholder in the repository is shorter than 16. Non-loopback binds with tokens print a plain-HTTP notice: the token, which is also the UI password, and the memories cross the network unencrypted, so outside a trusted network a TLS reverse proxy belongs in front. The README gains "Public deployment" (Caddy and nginx settings, `CUEMEM_ALLOWED_HOSTS`, rate limiting in the proxy), and its token example writes `openssl rand -hex 32` into `.env`, out of shell history. CI's docker job token was lengthened to 19 characters, since the container would otherwise refuse to start.
     - **Per-key limits** (P10): a scoped key limits whose memory a caller reaches, not what it spends, and any valid key could make the server call the operator's providers without limit (reproduced: 50 forwarded requests with `qwen3.8-max`, `n=16`, `max_tokens=32768` and `enable_thinking=true` from a key scoped to one user; user-less requests too). Keys in `CUEMEM_API_KEYS_FILE` / `CUEMEM_API_KEYS` may now set `rate_per_minute` (write requests, every method except GET, HEAD and OPTIONS, under `/api/`, `/v1/` and `/mcp`; a token bucket of that capacity refilled at rate/60 per second, checked before the body is read; beyond it 429 with `Retry-After`, code `rate_limited` under `/api/v1`), `models` (the proxy refuses other models with an OpenAI-format 403, type `permission_error`, param `model`, before any recall, session or forwarding; a listed alias admits only itself, a listed model id that is not itself an alias admits all its aliases, and for such keys the catalogue's `extra_body` overrides the caller's values such as `enable_thinking`) and `max_output_tokens` (the proxy clamps or sets `max_tokens` / `max_completion_tokens`, and an `n` other than 1 gets 400; on its own it does not pin `enable_thinking` or `thinking`, which only `models` replaces). Without these fields nothing changes, and forwarded bodies are byte-identical to the admin token's. The review found that the first version counted only POST (PUT `/api/v1/memories/{id}`, a synchronous extraction, and DELETE, a re-extraction, went through) and that `models: [deepseek-flash]` admitted `deepseek-flash-thinking`, a sibling alias of the same model id that turns thinking on; both are fixed.
     - **Qdrant API key** (P4): `vector_store.api_key` (`CUEMEM_VECTOR_STORE__API_KEY`, a secret; empty means none; read from the environment or `.env` only, with no CLI flag so it stays out of shell history) is sent as the `api-key` header by every CueMem process (serve, proxy, SDK, CLI) and by `cuemem migrate-vectors`; `Memory(config=...)` accepts it. Before, the key was silently ignored, so keyed servers and Qdrant Cloud answered 401, and the README's Qdrant (`docker run -p 6333:6333`) was reachable without authentication on every interface, bypassing CueMem's tokens and scopes. docker compose sets the qdrant service's `QDRANT__SERVICE__API_KEY` from the same variable (unset or empty: no authentication on either side, as before; that service publishes no port); the documented `docker run` publishes on 127.0.0.1 and keeps the data in a named volume. `cuemem doctor` shows whether the key is set; with an `http://` URL qdrant-client warns once per client. CI's `qdrant-server` job now runs against a keyed server, with the new test-only `CUEMEM_TEST_QDRANT_API_KEY` next to D-070's `CUEMEM_TEST_QDRANT_URL`.
     - **No third-party calls from the UI** (P6): the theme's font is the IBM Plex Sans that ships with Gradio (weights 400, 600 and 700, served by the app) instead of Google-hosted Inter, and `gr.Blocks(analytics_enabled=…)` follows `GRADIO_ANALYTICS_ENABLED == "True"`, read after `.env` is loaded. Before, every visitor's browser fetched fonts.googleapis.com, and a mounted UI sent Gradio's version check (api.gradio.app) and usage analytics (huggingface.co) on construction. `HF_HUB_DISABLE_TELEMETRY` alone would not have stopped Gradio's call, so the Dockerfile sets nothing.
     - **Non-ASCII identity headers** (P22): Starlette decodes headers as Latin-1, so curl's raw UTF-8 `张三` used to land in the space `å¼\xa0ä¸\x89` (200 with the admin token, 403 with a key scoped to 张三), and Node clients (Claude Code's `--header`) and httpx could not send it at all. `auth.header_id` now decodes `X-CueMem-User` and `X-CueMem-Agent` on `/mcp` and the proxy: a value starting with `utf-8'` (any case) as an RFC 8187 ext-value, ASCII unchanged, otherwise UTF-8 when the bytes are valid and Latin-1 when not (so the Latin-1 `José` that Node fetch and requests send keeps working; a 400 for every non-UTF-8 value was considered and dropped for that reason). A malformed `utf-8''` value, bytes that are not UTF-8, or a control character get 400 (`{"detail"}` on `/mcp`, an OpenAI-format `invalid_request_error` with param `user` on the proxy). The only change for ASCII values: one starting with `utf-8'` is decoded. These surfaces had not reached main, so no data migrates.
     - **Changed defaults:** a token-less non-loopback server reached by a DNS name (`nas.local`, a compose service name used by another container) must list that name in `CUEMEM_ALLOWED_HOSTS`; cross-site POSTs to `/login` get 403; public binds and Docker containers with a token shorter than 16 characters stop starting until the token is rotated or `--insecure-weak-token` is passed (in compose, added to `command:`); non-loopback binds with tokens print the plain-HTTP notice; the UI sends no Gradio telemetry or version check unless `GRADIO_ANALYTICS_ENABLED=True`, and its font is the local IBM Plex Sans instead of Google-hosted Inter; with `CUEMEM_VECTOR_STORE__API_KEY` in `.env`, compose's Qdrant requires it. New: `--insecure-weak-token` on `serve` and `proxy`; the setting `vector_store.api_key`; the key fields `rate_per_minute`, `models` and `max_output_tokens` (and the matching `Principal` fields); `MIN_TOKEN_CHARS`, `Keys.weak`, `auth.header_id` and `identity_headers`; `create_app(allow_ip_hosts=)`, `create_proxy_app(allow_ip_hosts=)` and `TrustedHosts(ip_literals=)`; `BodyLimit(clock=)`, `cuemem.api.limits.RATE_PATHS` and `READ_METHODS`; `cli._host_policy` returns a three-field `HostPolicy`.
     - **Left:** UI (cookie) sessions are not covered by the per-key limits (they use the agent role's model with `max_tokens` 1200); the size of one write is bounded only by `CUEMEM_MAX_BODY_BYTES`; provider fields a caller adds (`enable_search` and the like) pass through; a daily spending budget per key, with ledger rows stamped by principal, needs its own decision. CueMem does not rate-limit sign-ins or failed tokens (Alternatives).
     - **Evaluation:** evaluations go through none of these surfaces; without the new key fields the proxy forwards byte-identical bodies (tests compare them with the admin token's).
  3. **Sessions, runs and forget residue** (P3, P16, P12, P20; revises D-021, D-045, D-046, D-061, D-062, D-068 groups 2 and 3, D-069 groups 1 and 5, D-070 groups 1 and 2):
     - **Deletions reach open sessions** (P3): before, only `ChatService.forget` (`/api/spaces/{space}/forget`, the UI, MCP) pruned open sessions. An `/api/v1` delete, `delete_all` or reset (admin or scoped), the SDK and the integrations left the deleted fact in an open UI or `/api` session's profile snapshot; the next turn sent it to the model, and a reply that repeated it was written back to the log. Each space now has a forget counter (`MemorySystem.forget_epoch`), bumped inside the exclusive hold after a forget's commit, again after its replay finishes or fails (review), and after `delete_space`; each chat turn compares its session's counter with the space's inside its read hold and prunes first (`Agent._prune_locked`: drop the deleted rounds, invalidate deleted image labels, take the profile again). So a turn queued behind a forget, and a session started or pruned while the forget was rebuilding the profile, take the rebuilt profile. `ChatService.forget` still prunes at once, so the UI updates immediately.
     - **Runs across a restart** (P16): a run left open when `cuemem serve` stops or crashes survives the restart: `add(run_id=X)` continues it (`MemorySystem.resume_left_open`, as `open_run(X)` already did), so it is extracted once when it ends instead of being re-extracted with a whole-space replay on every add (3 adds across a restart: 4 extractions and 3 replays before, 1 and 0 now); `add(wait=True)` on it raises "still open" as before the restart. Under `cuemem serve` and `cuemem proxy`, no other request adopts the sessions an earlier process left open during the first idle period (`MemorySystem.defer_adoption`: another run's add, a UI or `/api` session start, a proxy request; `consolidate()` and `ingest()` skip them too, review), and `api.app._adopt_after` hands any that nobody continued to extraction once, one idle period (`--idle-minutes`, default 30) after start-up, even if nobody touches the space again (before, a restart alone adopted nothing). A run this process handed over and the client then continues is reopened like an idle-closed run (extracted again once and replayed once when it ends); these records live in memory, at most `HANDED_OVER_KEPT` = 10,000, and `delete_all` and `reset` drop them. The local SDK and CLI still adopt on first touch. `/api/v1` runs stay open across a graceful stop of `cuemem serve`, now on purpose.
     - **Cleanup after a forget's commit never raises** (P12): rewriting a daily trace file needs free space equal to its size, the commit only a few KB, so on a full disk the OSError used to skip the embedding-cache purge and the WAL scrub; `DELETE /api/v1/memories/{id}` answered 500, and a retry found nothing to delete, so the deleted text stayed in `traces/` and its question and document vectors in `embed.sqlite`. A trace line cut inside a UTF-8 character (a failed append followed by a good one) made every later forget on the server raise after its commit (HTTP 400) and stopped retention. Now a daily file that cannot be rewritten is deleted whole (deleting needs no space): other spaces' debug traces of that day are lost (traces are debug-only, D-046), one warning goes to `cuemem.core.trace`, and the query vectors of every query in that file are purged (recomputed on next use). A file that can be neither rewritten nor deleted, or cannot be read, is logged and named in the forget trace's `traces_left` (`["*"]` when the cleanup itself raised). Lines that are not UTF-8 are kept byte for byte and not parsed, and `TraceSink.write` truncates a failed append back to the file's previous length. The review added: when purging the embedding cache fails after the commit ("database or disk is full", or "database is locked" on the `embed.sqlite` a source checkout shares with evaluations), one warning goes to `cuemem.memory` and the forget trace records `embed_cache_left: true`; the forget or delete still succeeds (HTTP 200) and the purge is not retried; the same holds for the query-vector purge after `delete_all` and `reset` (`purge_space_traces`), and the WAL scrub is guarded the same way.
     - **Query vectors that forget missed** (P20): forget, `delete_all` / `reset` and trace retention now also purge the query vectors of L2's planned sub-queries, of the sufficiency checker's follow-up queries (the checker sees memory and can restate deleted content, for example "bank card PIN 4321 Commerzbank"), of the agent's own searches (ToolReader, and `Agent.chat`'s `memory_search`, now recorded as an `agent` event `{searches, queries}` in the chat trace) and (review) of the shop demo's catalogue searches (`search_products`; `searches` still counts only memory searches). These texts are read from what the traces already hold (`trace_queries`: `plan.sub_queries`, `check.queries`, `agent.queries`), so traces written before this change are covered. With the DashScope multimodal embedder, the multimodal text-query vectors (`mm|…|text|…`) of all these queries are purged too, and forget purges a deleted image's vector (`mm|…|image|…`) once no media row uses its sha256, the same rule as deleting the original file (`DashScopeMultimodalEmbedder.purge`; counted in `ForgetReport.embed_cache_purged`).
     - **The proxy's write-back race** (P20): the proxy records the space's forget count before recall. When a forget happened before the write-back (for example another client's DELETE while the upstream model answers, longer when streaming), an answer built on injected evidence is not written to the log, and `proxy_write_dropped` (reason `forgotten`, part `answer`) is recorded when a trace is active. The user's message is still written.
     - **Changed defaults:** open chat sessions re-sync on their next turn after any forget; serve and proxy hand over sessions an earlier process left open one idle period after start-up (this may send extraction requests then), so crash-left UI, `/api` and proxy sessions are extracted at start + idle rather than on the user's first request after the restart; `add(run_id=X)` on a run an earlier process left open reopens it instead of adopting it; a forget no longer raises from trace or cache cleanup after its commit, and a daily trace file that cannot be rewritten is deleted whole; forget and retention purge more query vectors; the proxy drops an answer built on memory forgotten meanwhile. New internals: `MemorySystem.forget_epoch`, `resume_left_open`, `adopt_left_open` and `defer_adoption`, `api.app._adopt_after`, `TraceSink.remove(..., dropped=, left=)`, `cuemem.core.trace.trace_queries`, `DashScopeMultimodalEmbedder.purge`, `ToolContext.queries`. No new flags, environment variables or endpoints.
     - **Left:** a run that the previous process had already closed for idleness (recorded only in that process's memory), or one handed over and no longer remembered (beyond 10,000 runs), is a plain session after a restart: each later add re-extracts it and replays the user's whole space (D-045), and the user's facts are missing from search and `get_all` during each replay; clients that pause for long should call `end_run` at natural breaks and open a new run. An optional `cuemem serve --run-idle-minutes` needs an owner decision. Query-image vectors of `cuemem search --image` are not purged unless the same image is stored; the cache entry of an image whose online write rolled back stays orphaned; Gradio's temporary copies can outlive a forget by up to about 2 hours (now in a private directory, group 4). The recall → model → write shape fixed in the proxy remains, unreproduced, in the three integrations.
     - **Accepted cost:** in a source checkout, where product and evaluations share `data/cache/embed.sqlite`, forgetting or opt-in retention can delete an evaluation's identical sub-query or multimodal query vector, which a replay then recomputes (needs network), as `prune_traces` already did for questions.
     - **Evaluation:** evaluations never forget, open chat sessions or runs, start the worker or go through serve or the proxy, and use `tracer=None`; the new `agent` event goes to traces only, which `n8_context` does not count (it counts `llm` and `embedding` events). The forget counter lives in process memory.
  4. **File permissions** (P14; revises D-056, D-060, D-068 group 2):
     - Before, with the usual umask 022, `data`, `data/cache`, `data/qdrant` and `runs/traces` were 0755 and every database, cache and day's trace 0644 (a checkout's data directory is the repository root, `/opt/cuemem` on a typical server), so another local account could read conversations, full extraction prompts, questions, exports and photos; the media store copied 0600 uploads to 0644, and the UI's gallery copied them into the machine-wide, 0755 `/tmp/gradio`; `cuemem configure` rewrote a 0644 `.env` in place before its chmod, so a descriptor opened earlier read the new keys.
     - The `cuemem` CLI now tightens its umask to at least 077 and never loosens it, so every file it writes is 0600 and every directory 0700 (database, caches, traces, `export -o`, evaluation results, uploads, downloaded models). `MemorySystem.from_settings`, and so the SDK, creates missing storage directories top-down at 0700 (`cuemem.store.files.private_dirs` and `storage_dirs`: the database, ledger, both caches, traces, media and the local vector store); it is best-effort and never chmods a directory that already exists. `cuemem serve` with the UI uses a private per-start Gradio temporary directory (`mkdtemp`, 0700), deleted on exit, unless `GRADIO_TEMP_DIR` is set. `cuemem configure` writes `.env` through a 0600 temporary file and `os.replace` (a symlinked `.env` keeps its link; the directory holding it must be writable). On POSIX, `cuemem doctor` warns about group- or world-accessible data directories (`chmod 700 <dir>`, the top-most one only), `.env` and `CUEMEM_API_KEYS_FILE` (`chmod 600 <file>`), and `serve` and `proxy` warn about the two secret files at start-up; nothing is changed for the operator.
     - **Changed defaults:** CLI files 0600 and new directories 0700, so an operator relying on group read access (a backup user, say) must grant it explicitly; `.env` is a new inode after each `configure`; `GRADIO_TEMP_DIR` is honoured and documented as an override; compose and `.env.example` say `install -m 600 .env.example .env`. No new flags.
     - **Evaluation:** modes only; no request, cache key or file content changes.
  5. **Installing and the first run** (P15, P19; revises D-060, D-068 group 7, D-069 group 3):
     - **Install from GitHub** (P15; owner decision: not on PyPI for now): the PyPI name `cuemem` is unregistered, yet the CLI hints and four doc lines said `pip install "cuemem[…]"`, which resolves against PyPI, so a fresh environment, `-U`, pipx or uv tool installs and the image's system pip could install a squatter's package (simulated offline with two indexes). `cuemem.config.INSTALL_URL = "git+https://github.com/FlickYan/cuemem"` (to be set to None after a PyPI release); outside a checkout `extra_remedy()` returns `pip install "cuemem[<extra>] @ git+https://github.com/FlickYan/cuemem"`, and the missing-fastembed error uses the same helper; inside the repository's Docker image (review: `/opt/venv` without pip, and no git) it returns `docker build --build-arg EXTRAS="--extra ui --extra local[ --extra <x>]" -t cuemem .`. The documents say CueMem is not on PyPI yet. `pyproject.toml` gains keywords and `[project.urls]`. History lines in earlier ADRs and the roadmap are not rewritten; D-069's is amended.
     - **One-line configuration errors** (P19): every `load_settings` call in the CLI (15 sites, `_open_system` and `_service` included) goes through `_settings()`, so an unknown preset or an invalid `CUEMEM_*` value prints `错误：配置有误：<field>：<reason>（收到 <value>）` and exits 1 (on stderr for `cuemem mcp`) instead of 49- and 72-line tracebacks; fields that look like secrets, and whole configuration mappings, are not echoed (merge fix, so a mistyped Qdrant key never reaches a log). The SDK still raises ValueError or ValidationError. D-068 group 7's one-line errors did not change the exit code, as a traceback also exits 1, so compose's `restart: unless-stopped` still restarts the container on a configuration error. The policy stays (it recovers from crashes and reboots); the README, `.env.example` and `docker-compose.yml` now say how to see the error (`docker compose ps` shows Restarting, `docker compose logs cuemem` the one-line reason).
     - **`.env` and Docker** (P19): `.env.example`'s trailing comments moved to their own lines, because `docker run --env-file` keeps ` # …` in the value (compose and python-dotenv strip it); the README, `.env.example`, compose and the Dockerfile say which keys each configuration needs and that Docker needs `CUEMEM_API_TOKEN`.
     - **A keyless trial** (P19): the missing-embedding-key hint now names a separate `CUEMEM_HOME`, since a data directory remembers its embedding model even after a failed command; `examples/keyless.py` writes and retrieves with hash embeddings in a temporary directory, using `with`, because a plain script that does not close `Memory` prints a harmless Qdrant "Exception ignored" traceback at exit; the README shows it and an offline curl path; an English `examples/README.md` lists every example with its keys and command, and the OpenAI Agents example now runs in `CUEMEM_HOME=$(mktemp -d)`.
     - **Changed defaults:** outside a checkout every missing-extra hint is the GitHub form, and in the image a rebuild; configuration errors print one line; the missing-embedding-key message changed (shown to people only). New `cuemem.config.INSTALL_URL` and `docker_image()`, `examples/keyless.py` and `examples/README.md`; the wheel metadata gains Keywords and Project-URL. No new flags or environment variables; `restart: unless-stopped` is unchanged.
     - **Left (owner):** the identity for `authors` and `CITATION.cff`; optional CONTRIBUTING, pull-request and issue templates; a `v0.1.0` tag after this round merges; the repository's description, topics and homepage.
  6. **Licences, notices and claims** (P8, P9, P11, P17; revises D-010, D-048, D-065, D-067, D-069 group 4, D-070 group 7):
     - **Notices** (P8): `THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.md` said the generated same-topic questions in `evals/mixed/` do not copy benchmark text. In fact 4 test rows repeat their source question verbatim (LoCoMo conv-43-q71 and conv-47-q36, LongMemEval gpt4_1a1dc16d and e493bb7c), and 7 development and 33 test rows are near copies (difflib ratio ≥ 0.8; the 295 MemLens rows could not be checked). The notices now say `evals/mixed/` is not covered by CueMem's MIT License, with the per-source attribution CC 4.0 §3(a)(1) requires: LoCoMo (Maharana et al., ACL 2024, snap-research/locomo at 3eb6f2c, CC BY-NC 4.0, as is, modified by an LLM, non-commercial use only), MemLens (Ren et al. 2026, CC BY 4.0) and LongMemEval (MIT); a three-line `evals/mixed/README.md` says the same. A-MEM's keyword prompt (`amem.py`'s `QUERY_PROMPT`, from WujiangXu/AgenticMemory at f9a173d, MIT) gets its notice and copyright line; the MemLens judge prompts are described as copied from upstream `judge_prompts/` (MIT, and attributed under CC BY 4.0 as well); the LongMemEval test fixture as reformatted by ruff. The data files and prompts are byte-for-byte unchanged. The report adds the known flaw: those rows are labelled as needing no memory, each moving the topic AUC (0.984) and the false-recall rates by at most 1/1,750.
     - **N9 tables and figures** (P9): `tables.md`'s header now says costs are answer-only (the one-off write, about $43 / $35 / $15 / $54 per 1,000 histories on LongMemEval / LoCoMo / MemLens 32K / 128K, excluded) and that the LongMemEval methods other than B0, L0, B1 and ORACLE predate the D-067 fix, and a "Holm 校正后" column from `results-holm.json` shows the 21 comparisons that are nominal only (249 rows with p, 228 adjusted, 21 at +0 / −0 shown as "—"). The Pareto figure's x-axis says "nominal answer cost … one-off write excluded", and full context, which was silently left off the plot on LongMemEval ($31.88) and MemLens 128K ($40.60), is drawn at the right edge with its numbers; the plots use matplotlib's default style and DejaVu Sans, so a machine's matplotlibrc no longer changes them. `scripts/n9_report.py` used to overwrite the published `results.json` (127,088 → 138 bytes), `results-holm.json` and `tables.md` on a machine without the N9 runs; it now exits 1 and writes nothing, `--figures-only` redraws from the published JSON, and `reproduce.py` declares the runs that step requires. The JSON files are byte-identical.
     - **External baselines** (P11): the abstracts said L1 and L2 stay significantly higher than A-MEM with k = 40. L2 does (+32 / −8, p < 0.001), but L1's +27 / −12 (p = 0.024) is only nominal: it is the largest p of its 3-test sensitivity family and so not corrected there at all, and 0.19 in the whole-document family (0.095 if k = 40 replaced k = 10 in the 10-test family, recomputed with `routing.holm` from the published wins and losses). The families were not redefined: that would move the cited L2-vs-Mem0 adjusted p under pds from 0.068 to 0.085. The summaries put Graphiti's and LangMem's drop under Qwen down to their writes only; they now also name the read-side factors of the shared harness that were not measured separately (Graphiti's 0.6 threshold not retuned for Qwen embeddings; LangMem's long memories past the embedder's 6,000-character input, 12 of 123 under pq and 2 of 705 under pds), say that pds runs Graphiti's basic search (no reranker, no BFS), and list the adapters' departures from upstream (report §13.4, both languages). The verifier found that the raised retrieval budgets of Mem0 and LangMem, A-MEM's patches and the self-judging judge under pds are not handicaps; they are listed neutrally.
     - **The video and the per-node records** (P17): the 60-second video's source no longer says "1/20 of the cost" or "test sets used once" (now "1/20 the answer-time cost" and "one frozen pass over the test sets"); the uploaded video still does until the owner re-renders it, uploads it as a new video (YouTube cannot replace one in place) and updates its 8 links, and the README notes under the thumbnail that its costs are answer-time costs and its LongMemEval numbers predate the leak fix. The hand-written N3, N4, N5, N7, N9 and N11 records, and the Chinese README's results, now carry the D-067 leak note (N7: the controller's accuracy tables and λ were fitted on results from the leaky period and not refitted; N11: after the fix, L1 90.0% and Mem0 79.0% on the same 100 questions). The generated `report.md` files are not edited, since `reproduce.py` overwrites them.
     - **Evaluation:** documents, notices, one generated table, two figures and the script that writes them; no accuracy, win, loss, raw or adjusted p, or JSON changed.
  7. **Reproducibility of the external baselines** (P21; revises D-055, D-065, D-070 group 7):
     - Mem0's extra packages are pinned (`--with spacy==3.8.16 --with fastembed==0.9.0`, the versions in the N12 build machine's uv cache; unpinned they now resolve fastembed 0.9.1), and A-MEM's evaluation command gains `--with chromadb==1.5.9`, in the workflow, `reproduce.py`, the scripts, the docstrings and the N12 records.
     - `pds-locomo.json` and `pq-locomo.json` gain new keys only (otherwise byte-identical; the `-holm.json` files compare equal): `methods.<m>.retrieval` (session and turn recall; null turn recall for M0 and ML, whose results carry no turn ids; no key for B1) and, for the external systems, `methods.<m>.build` (Graphiti's entities, facts and warnings, LangMem's empty sessions and over-long memories, Cognee's nodes and edges, A-MEM's links and evolutions, Mem0's failed sessions), so every recall and build figure the docs cite has a source.
     - `evals.external.installed_environment()` is written into each new external build's `build.json` as `environment`. The published builds did not record transitive versions: `docs/results/n12-baselines/environments/<system>.txt` keeps the environments found in the build machine's uv cache, which show that some transitive versions changed within the build window (langchain 1.4.6 / 1.4.3, neo4j 6.4.0 / 6.4.1, transformers 5.19.0 / 5.20.0). They are records, not lock files.
     - **Left:** the "外部基线" workflow (D-070) has not run on GitHub yet; its first run is expected on the pull request that merges this round, and its result belongs in this record. A fully locked install of the baselines in CI is left for later, to be set from these lists rather than from today's resolution.
     - **Evaluation:** the pins change only the external baselines' `uv run --with` overlays, and `environment` is written after the build; default and evaluation requests, cache keys and the frozen methods are unchanged.
- **Alternatives:**
  - **Pillow 13 without the two-call resize:** it changes the bytes of every resized photo taller than about 2:1 (127 of 305 random sizes in the verifier's sweep), and so the cache keys of caption, multimodal and reader requests for such MemLens and N6 photos. **Restricting formats inside `image_data_url`:** stored evaluation media in other formats would stop replaying.
  - **Digest-pinning the base images now** (P5, P18): an unverified digest would break the required docker check, and without Dependabot proposing new digests the image would stop picking up security patches. **`[tool.uv] required-version`:** it breaks every uv command on machines still on uv 0.11.32; adopt after updating.
  - **An IP-keyed failed-sign-in throttle** (P13): behind Docker port publishing or a proxy container every visitor shares one client IP, so it would let anyone lock everyone out; long tokens make online guessing infeasible, and rate limiting belongs in the reverse proxy.
  - **For spending** (P10): forcing a user on scoped proxy requests (a key with its own user spends the same, and D-069 group 6's pass-through stays); refusing `n`, `store`, `service_tier` and similar fields (provider-dependent, breaks drop-in clients); a global `CUEMEM_RATE_PER_MINUTE`; a daily budget in this round (it needs ledger rows stamped by principal).
  - **A 400 for every header value that is not UTF-8** (P22): Node fetch and requests send `José` as Latin-1 today.
  - **Pruning sessions from inside `forget()`** (P3): `delete_space` re-enters forget, and the prune would wait for a session lock held by a turn that waits for the read gate (reproduced as a deadlock; a regression test now times out on that design).
  - **Adopting left-open runs at start-up** (P16): a run continued after a deploy would re-extract and replay on every add again. **Persisting the idle-closed handover** to cover runs closed by the previous process: new user-identifying metadata to clean on every forget path, for a narrow case.
  - **A pending-purge row for failed cache purges** (P12): it would store the deleted text again (D-021).
  - **Dropping the user's message too in the proxy race** (P20): consistent with D-069's "messages from before a forget are never written" and covering a `delete_all` racing a message, but it would lose a legitimate message whenever an unrelated fact is forgotten mid-request.
  - **`chmod 700 /data` in the image** (P14): the volume sits under 0710 `/var/lib/docker`, the container has a single uid, and bind mounts ignore the image directory's mode.
  - **Closing `Memory` at interpreter exit** (P19): ending runs and the worker at exit could delay or hang it while a provider call is in flight, the close path is not idempotent, and `Memory.from_system` wrappers must never close a system they do not own. **`restart: on-failure`:** it gives up after a few seconds of doubling retries, and `unless-stopped` also recovers from crashes and reboots.
  - **Redefining the Holm families** so that A-MEM with k = 40 is in the 10-test family (P11): it would move adjusted p values already cited.
- **Consequences:**
  - Code and tests: 77 files changed (not counting docs), 78 new test functions; 1,225 tests collected, 6 live tests deselected, 1,219 run offline, of which 23 are skipped without the external baselines' packages (18) or a Qdrant server (5, one more than in D-070: the check that a keyed server refuses an unkeyed client) and run in CI instead; ruff and mypy pass. Every behaviour fix was checked to fail on the code before it.
  - **Evaluation requests, image requests and published numbers are unchanged:** `FROZEN_REQUESTS` in `tests/test_eval_leak.py`, `tests/test_frozen_config.py` and the golden cache-key tests in `tests/test_llm_client.py` and `tests/test_embedding.py` are byte-for-byte the files of the round's base and pass, and none was re-pinned; the default consolidation fingerprint stays `2f0adc7890b6a52e`; image request bytes are pinned by the new goldens in `tests/test_store.py` and pass on Pillow 12.3.0 and 13.0.0. As in D-070, what is pinned is request bytes and cache keys; the N2–N11 replay was not re-run.
  - **Requests that change on purpose:** none on the default or evaluation paths. The proxy changes a forwarded body only for keys that set `models` or `max_output_tokens`; serve and proxy may send extraction requests one idle period after start-up for sessions an earlier process left open.
  - **History:** the repository history was rewritten once before it went public, so commit hashes cited in older notes changed; the hashes in the docs and result records were updated, while `run.json` files in a local `runs/` directory still carry the old ones.
  - **Left for later or partial:**
    1. From P1: on a machine with the MemLens and N6 photos, compare `image_data_url` digests under Pillow 12.3.0 (single resize) and 13.0.0 (two calls), with identical digests expected; pip installs of `cuemem[ui]` and `cuemem[local]` stay on Pillow 12 until gradio and fastembed lift their caps;
    2. From P5 and P18: the repository settings (private reporting, alerts, security updates, push protection, importing the ruleset) are the owner's; digest-pinning the base images together with Dependabot, `required-version`, and restricting `push` to main are owner choices;
    3. From P10 and P13: a daily spending budget per key; UI sessions, the size of one write and caller-added provider fields are outside the per-key limits; sign-ins are not rate-limited by CueMem;
    4. From P15: the identity for `authors` and `CITATION.cff`, contributor templates, a `v0.1.0` tag and the repository's About settings;
    5. From P16: runs closed for idleness by the previous process (group 3); `--run-idle-minutes`;
    6. From P17: re-rendering and re-uploading the video, and updating its links;
    7. From P20 and D-068: query-image vectors of `cuemem search --image`, the orphaned cache entry of a rolled-back image write, Gradio's temporary copies for up to about 2 hours, and the recall → model → write shape in the three integrations;
    8. From P21: the first run of the "外部基线" workflow, and a fully locked install of the baselines;
    9. D-070's items 1, 3 and 6 are done except the amortised write-cost column of `tables.md` (needs the N9 runs) and guarding test subprocesses; its items 2, 4, 5, 7 and 8 are unchanged.
  - **Remaining limits:**
    - on a token-less public bind any IP literal is accepted as Host (that is the trusted network `--insecure-no-auth` is for), and the server still speaks plain HTTP;
    - adopting left-open sessions, per-session turn numbers and orphan-vector deletion still assume one process writes a database at a time (as in D-068);
    - a rollback still bumps the database's turn epoch (D-070), which conservatively tells pending proxy and Agents write-backs, and now the proxy's answer check, that a forget happened.
